This is a critical vulnerability that allows attackers to execute arbitrary system commands remotely **without authentication**.\n\n### Vulnerability Overview\n\nThe vulnerability exists due to a **message routing flaw in the SSH state machine** where SSH Connection Protocol messages (`ssh_msg_channel_*`) can be processed during the authentication phase (`{userauth, server}` state) without verifying that the user has been authenticated.\n\n---\n\n## 1. SSH Server Architecture\n\n### Key Files:\n- `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl` - Main SSH connection state machine (gen_statem)\n- `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_fsm_userauth_server.erl` - Server-side authentication FSM handler\n- `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection.erl` - SSH Connection Protocol implementation\n\n### Architecture:\nThe SSH server uses a hierarchical state machine approach:\n1. **Transport Layer** (`ssh_transport.erl`) - Handles SSH protocol encryption/decryption\n2. **Authentication Layer** (`ssh_fsm_userauth_server.erl`) - Delegates to the main handler\n3. **Connection Layer** (`ssh_connection.erl`) - Processes SSH Connection Protocol messages\n\n---\n\n## 2. The Vulnerability Chain\n\n### Stage 1: Message Decryption (ssh_connection_handler.erl, lines 1160-1177)\n\nThe main connection handler routes all decrypted SSH protocol messages in the `handle_event` function:\n\n```erlang\n% File: /app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl\n% Lines: 1160-1177\n\n#ssh_msg_global_request{}            = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n#ssh_msg_channel_open{}              = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n#ssh_msg_channel_request{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n#ssh_msg_channel_success{}           = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n```\n\nThe `?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)` macro (line 577) sends these messages to the internal connection handler:\n\n```erlang\n-define(CONNECTION_MSG(Msg),\n        [{next_event, internal, prepare_next_packet},\n         {next_event,internal,{conn_msg,Msg}}]).\n```\n\n**Critical Issue**: These messages are routed regardless of the current state (including during `{userauth, server}` state).\n\n---\n\n### Stage 2: Message Delegation in Userauth State (ssh_fsm_userauth_server.erl, lines 153-154)\n\nThe userauth server FSM has a **catch-all default handler**:\n\n```erlang\n% File: /app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_fsm_userauth_server.erl\n% Lines: 152-154\n\n%%% ######## UNHANDLED EVENT!\nhandle_event(Type, Event, StateName, D) ->\n    ssh_connection_handler:handle_event(Type, Event, StateName, D).\n```\n\nThis handler **delegates ANY unhandled message** (including `{conn_msg, Msg}` for channel operations) back to the main connection handler while **still in the `{userauth, server}` state**.\n\n---\n\n### Stage 3: Connection Message Processing (ssh_connection_handler.erl, lines 759-801)\n\nThe connection handler processes these delegated messages:\n\n```erlang\n% File: /app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl\n% Lines: 759-801\n\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg,Msg}, StateName, #data{connection_state = Connection0,\n                                                        event_queue = Qev0} = D0) ->\n    Role = ?role(StateName),\n    Rengotation = renegotiation(StateName),\n    try ssh_connection:handle_msg(Msg, Connection0, Role, D0#data.ssh_params) of\n        {Replies, Connection} when is_list(Replies) ->\n            {Repls, D} = \n                case StateName of\n                    {connected,_} ->\n                        send_replies(Replies, D0#data{connection_state=Connection});\n                    _ ->\n                        %% Non-connected state - messages are queued!\n                        {ConnReplies, NonConnReplies} = lists:splitwith(fun not_connected_filter/1, Replies),\n                        send_replies(NonConnReplies, D0#data{event_queue = Qev0 ++ ConnReplies})\n                end,\n```\n\nThis calls `ssh_connection:handle_msg()` which **does NOT check if the user is authenticated**.\n\n---\n\n### Stage 4: Channel Command Execution (ssh_connection.erl)\n\nThe ssh_connection module processes channel requests without authentication verification:\n\n#### 4a. Session Channel Opening (lines 856-884)\n\n```erlang\n% File: /app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection.erl\n% Lines: 856-884\n\nhandle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_open{channel_type = \"session\" = Type,\n                                 sender_channel = RemoteId,\n                                 initial_window_size = WindowSz,\n                                 maximum_packet_size = PacketSz}, \n           #connection{options = SSHopts} = Connection0,\n           server, _SSH) ->\n    MinAcceptedPackSz = ?GET_OPT(minimal_remote_max_packet_size, SSHopts),\n    if \n        MinAcceptedPackSz =< PacketSz ->\n            try setup_session(Connection0, RemoteId,\n                          Type, WindowSz, PacketSz) of\n                Result -> Result\n```\n\n**NO AUTHENTICATION CHECK** - the session channel is opened immediately.\n\n#### 4b. Shell Request Execution (lines 1135-1140)\n\n```erlang\n% File: /app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection.erl\n% Lines: 1135-1140\n\nhandle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_request{recipient_channel = ChannelId,\n                                    request_type = \"shell\",\n                                    want_reply = WantReply},\n           Connection, server, _SSH) ->\n    handle_cli_msg(Connection, ChannelId,\n                   {shell, ChannelId, WantReply});\n```\n\n**NO AUTHENTICATION CHECK** - shell is started immediately.\n\n#### 4c. Command Execution (lines 1147-1154)\n\n```erlang\n% File: /app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection.erl\n% Lines: 1147-1154\n\nhandle_msg(#ssh_msg_channel_request{recipient_channel = ChannelId,\n                                    request_type = \"exec\",\n                                    want_reply = WantReply,\n                                    data = Data},\n           Connection, server, _SSH) ->\n    <<?DEC_BIN(Command, _Len)>> = Data,\n    handle_cli_msg(Connection, ChannelId,\n                   {exec, ChannelId, WantReply, binary_to_list(Command)});\n```\n\n**NO AUTHENTICATION CHECK** - commands are executed immediately.\n\n#### 4d. All channel/command handlers accept messages regardless of authentication state.\n\n---\n\n## 4. This means during the `{userauth, server}` state (before authentication), connection protocol messages like `channel_open` and `channel_request` are processed.\n\n3. **The vulnerability**: An attacker can send SSH connection protocol messages (like opening a channel and requesting command execution) before completing authentication, and these will be processed.\n\nThe fix should be in `ssh_fsm_userauth_server.erl` to explicitly reject connection protocol messages during the authentication phase instead of delegating them to the handler.\n\nLet me check what connection message types need to be blocked."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":230,"cache_read_input_tokens":28390,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":230,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e9c352d5-604d-48d8-9a77-660aa1a4cba4","uuid":"1ab534ed-4175-49c1-b2d5-2318588b4f4a"}

Protocol (RFC 4253), Authentication\n%% Protocol (RFC 4252) and SSH connection Protocol (RFC 4255)\n%% Details of the different protocols are\n%% implemented in ssh_transport.erl, ssh_auth.erl and ssh_connection.erl\n%% ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n-module(ssh_fsm_userauth_client).\n-moduledoc Exports\n%%====================================================================\n\n%%% Behaviour callbacks\n-export([callback_mode/0, handle_event/4, terminate/3,\n\t format_status/2, code_change/4]).\n\n%%====================================================================\n%% gen_statem callbacks\n%%====================================================================\n\ncallback_mode() ->\n    [handle_event_function,\n     state_enter].\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n%%% ######## {userauth, client} ####\n\n%%---- #ssh_msg_ext_info could follow after the key exchange, both the initial and the re-negotiation\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_ext_info{}=Msg, {userauth,client}, D0) ->\n    %% FIXME: need new state to receive this msg!\n    D = ssh_connection_handler:handle_ssh_msg_ext_info(Msg, D0),\n    {keep_state, D};\n\n%%---- received userauth success from the server\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_userauth_success{}, {userauth,client}, D0=#data{ssh_params = Ssh}) ->\n    ssh_auth:ssh_msg_userauth_result(success),\n    ssh_connection_handler:handshake(ssh_connected, D0),\n    D = D0#data{ssh_params=Ssh#ssh{authenticated = true}},\n    {next_state, {connected,client}, D, {change_callback_module,ssh_connection_handler}};\n\n\n%%---- userauth failure response to clientfrom the server\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_userauth_failure{}, {userauth,client}=StateName,\n\t     #data{ssh_params = #ssh{userauth_methods = []}} = D0) ->\n    {Shutdown, D} =\n        ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_NO_MORE_AUTH_METHODS_AVAILABLE, \n                         io_lib:format(\"User auth failed for: ~p\",[D0#data.auth_user]),\n                         StateName, D0),\n    {stop, Shutdown, D};\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_userauth_failure{authentications = Methods}, StateName={userauth,client},\n\t     D0 = #data{ssh_params = Ssh0}) ->\n    %% The preferred authentication method failed, try next method\n    Ssh1 = case Ssh0#ssh.userauth_methods of\n\t       none ->\n\t\t   %% Server tells us which authentication methods that are allowed\n\t\t   Ssh0#ssh{userauth_methods = string:tokens(Methods, \",\")};\n\t       _ ->\n\t\t   %% We already know...\n\t\t   Ssh0 \n\t   end,\n    case ssh_auth:userauth_request_msg(Ssh1) of\n        {send_disconnect, Code, Ssh} ->\n            {Shutdown, D} =\n                ?send_disconnect(Code, \n                                 io_lib:format(\"User auth failed for: ~p\",[D0#data.auth_user]),\n                                 StateName, D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh}),\n\t    {stop, Shutdown, D};\n\t{\"keyboard-interactive\", {Msg, Ssh}} ->\n            D = ssh_connection_handler:send_msg(Msg, D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh}),\n\t    {next_state, {userauth_keyboard_interactive,client}, D};\n\t{_Method, {Msg, Ssh}} ->\n            D = ssh_connection_handler:send_msg(Msg, D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh}),\n\t    {keep_state, D}\n    end;\n\n%%---- banner to client\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_userauth_banner{message = Msg}, {S,client}, D)\n  when S == userauth; S == userauth_keyboard_interactive;\n       S == userauth_keyboard_interactive_extra;\n       S == userauth_keyboard_interactive_info_response ->\n    case D#data.ssh_params#ssh.userauth_quiet_mode of\n\tfalse -> io:format(\"~s\", [Msg]);\n\ttrue -> ok\n    end,\n    keep_state_and_data;\n\n\n%%% ######## {userauth_keyboard_interactive, client}\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_userauth_info_request{} = Msg, {userauth_keyboard_interactive, client},\n\t     #data{ssh_params = Ssh0} = D0) ->\n    case ssh_auth:handle_userauth_info_request(Msg, Ssh0) of\n\t{ok, {Reply, Ssh}} ->\n            D = ssh_connection_handler:send_msg(Reply, D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh}),\n\t    {next_state, {userauth_keyboard_interactive_info_response,client}, D};\n\tnot_ok ->\n\t    {next_state, {userauth,client}, D0, [postpone]}\n    end;\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_userauth_failure{}, {userauth_keyboard_interactive, client},\n\t     #data{ssh_params = Ssh0} = D0) ->\n    Prefs = [{Method,M,F,A} || {Method,M,F,A} <- Ssh0#ssh.userauth_preference,\n\t\t\t       Method =/= \"keyboard-interactive\"],\n    D = D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh0#ssh{userauth_preference=Prefs}},\n    {next_state, {userauth,client}, D, [postpone]};\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_userauth_failure{}, {userauth_keyboard_interactive_info_response, client},\n\t     #data{ssh_params = Ssh0} = D0) ->\n    Opts = Ssh0#ssh.opts,\n    D = case ?GET_OPT(password, Opts) of\n\t    undefined ->\n\t\tD0;\n\t    _ ->\n\t\tD0#data{ssh_params =\n\t\t\t    Ssh0#ssh{opts = ?PUT_OPT({password,not_ok}, Opts)}} % FIXME:intermodule dependency\n\tend,\n    {next_state, {userauth,client}, D, [postpone]};\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_ext_info{}=Msg, {userauth_keyboard_interactive_info_response, client}, D0) ->\n    %% FIXME: need new state to receive this msg!\n    D = ssh_connection_handler:handle_ssh_msg_ext_info(Msg, D0),\n    {keep_state, D};\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_userauth_success{}, {userauth_keyboard_interactive_info_response, client}, D) ->\n    {next_state, {userauth,client}, D, [postpone]};\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_userauth_info_request{}, {userauth_keyboard_interactive_info_response, client}, D) ->\n    {next_state, {userauth_keyboard_interactive,client}, D, [postpone]};\n\n\n%%% ######## UNHANDLED EVENT!\nhandle_event(Type, Event, StateName, D) ->\n    ssh_connection_handler:handle_event(Type, Event, StateName, D).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\nformat_status(A, B) ->\n    ssh_connection_handler:format_status(A, B).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\nterminate(Reason, StateName, D) ->\n    ssh_connection_handler:terminate(Reason, StateName, {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_016VmaGa3QW2NWzPMYM4C55u","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The fix has been successfully implemented in both files. Here's a summary of the vulnerability and the fix:\n\n## Vulnerability Summary\n\n**CVE Type**: Pre-authentication Remote Command Execution\n\n**Root Cause**: The Erlang/OTP SSH server had a flaw in its state machine where SSH Connection Protocol messages (like `ssh_msg_channel_open`, `ssh_msg_channel_request` for \"exec\"/\"shell\", etc.) could be processed during the `{userauth, server}` state before the user completed authentication.\n\n**Attack Flow**:\n1. Attacker connects to SSH server and completes key exchange\n2. Server transitions to the `{userauth, server}` state waiting for authentication\n3. Instead of sending authentication messages, attacker sends:\n   - `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` (type \"session\") to open a channel\n   - `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` (type \"exec\" or \"shell\") to execute commands\n4. These messages were routed as `{conn_msg, Msg}` internal events\n5. The catch-all handler in `ssh_fsm_userauth_server.erl` delegated them to `ssh_connection_handler:handle_event/4`\n6. The connection handler processed them without checking authentication status\n7. Commands were executed on the server without any authentication\n\n## Fix Applied\n\nAdded explicit handlers in both `ssh_fsm_userauth_server.erl` and `ssh_fsm_userauth_client.erl` that intercept `{conn_msg, Msg}` events **before** the catch-all handler:\n\n- **File**: `lib/ssh/src/ssh_fsm_userauth_server.erl` (lines 152-164)\n- **File**: `lib/ssh/src/ssh_fsm_userauth_client.erl` (lines 163-175)\n\nThe fix:\n1. Matches any `{conn_msg, Msg}` internal event during the userauth phase\n2. Sends an `SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR` message to the client\n3. Terminates the connection with an appropriate shutdown\n\nThis ensures that SSH Connection Protocol messages received before authentication is complete are treated as protocol violations and result in immediate disconnection, preventing unauthenticated command execution."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1525,"cache_read_input_tokens":39175,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1525,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e9c352d5-604d-48d8-9a77-660aa1a4cba4","uuid":"78145d36-d22a-498a-b22f-f0e34e8d44f0"}
